Trace summary
0.346 final reward

Submission

The agent submitted a looped transformer that alternates between two width-512 blocks, applying each block 16 times. Each block uses width-256 attention and a width-608 SwiGLU feed-forward layer, fitting 32 block applications into the compute budget. Learned gains and input coefficients let each application adjust its update. Each block feeds a different half of the original token embedding directly into its attention values.

The agent used SOAP for the shared matrices and AdamW for the embedding and one-dimensional parameters. It trained on batches of 32 windows of up to 512 tokens each.

Submitted files in the run environment

  • /workspace/submission/config.json (505 B)
  • /workspace/submission/model.py (5.5 KB)
  • /workspace/submission/train.py (5.3 KB)

Scientific process

  • Repeat the optimizer comparison at full budget. On the starter architecture, Muon beat AdamW at 50M tokens and lost at 250M. The agent compared both optimizers again on a stronger shared block. Full-budget optimizer result
  • Compare optimizers at the same depth. With token embeddings added to the attention values, eight passes with SOAP nearly matched 16 passes with AdamW at about half the inference compute. At the same 16 passes, SOAP brought dev loss from 3.475 to 3.459. Depth comparison · 16-pass optimizer comparison
  • Inspect what the per-pass gains learned. In the 16-pass model, later passes gave more weight to the original token representation in attention. The direct contribution of the original input changed sign across passes. Learned gains
  • Revisit two blocks. The first two-block model lost to the one-block model. A later version with learned gains and smaller batches beat two one-block recipes, one tuned for batch size and one for the learning rate schedule. Over two seeds per recipe, it averaged 3.423 dev loss versus about 3.428 for each alternative. Final comparison

"""The starting model: a small dense GPT-2-style language model.

Four transformer blocks (width 256, 4 heads, causal attention with RoPE on
queries and keys, GELU-tanh MLP of width 896), each used once per token. The
model returns the final hidden states; the harness turns them into logits
with the token embedding table (tied output head). 2,896,928 unique weights besides
the 8192 x 256 embedding (not counted), about 12M FLOPs per token.

Initialisation: weights N(0, 0.02), biases 0, and every block's output
projections (attention and MLP c_proj) N(0, 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5


class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * cfg.d_model)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))


class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.c_fc = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.mlp_width)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.c_proj(F.gelu(self.c_fc(x), approximate="tanh"))


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln_1 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))


def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)


class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version

    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        for block in self.blocks:
            h = block(h, cos, sin)
        return h

    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head


def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""

    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
"""Starting training recipe: plain next-token loss.

Random 512-token windows, AdamW (betas 0.9, 0.95; weight decay on matrices
only), bf16 autocast, gradient clipping at 1, a linear warmup over the first
5% of the budget and a cosine decay to 10% of the learning rate over the
rest. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    if cfg["compile"] and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        model.inner.body.stack = torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params = list(model.parameters())
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([{"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() >= 2], "weight_decay": cfg["weight_decay"]},
                                   {"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() < 2], "weight_decay": 0.0}],
                                  lr=cfg["learning_rate"], betas=(0.9, 0.95), fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")

    def lr_at(progress: float) -> float:
        lr, warmup = cfg["learning_rate"], cfg["warmup"]
        if progress < warmup:
            return lr * max(progress, 1e-4) / warmup
        p = (progress - warmup) / (1 - warmup)
        return lr * (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, p))))

    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
            group["lr"] = lr_at(budget.fraction_used)
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(cfg["accumulation"]):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(cfg["micro_batch"])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / cfg["accumulation"]).backward()
            total += loss.item() / cfg["accumulation"]
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if not math.isfinite(total):
            raise FloatingPointError(f"loss is {total} at update {update}")
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": total, "lr": optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]})
{
  "model": {},
  "train": {
    "learning_rate": 0.002,
    "weight_decay": 0.1,
    "micro_batch": 32,
    "accumulation": 2,
    "warmup": 0.05,
    "compile": true,
    "log_every": 100
  }
}

"""What a submission provides.

submission/model.py defines

    def build_model(config: dict) -> nn.Module

where config is submission/config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...}. The model
maps token ids to hidden states, and must have its token embedding table as
the attribute embed = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, width):

    hidden = model(input_ids)              # [B, L] -> [B, L, width]

The harness owns the output layer: it wraps the model in
harness.OutputHead, which computes the next-token logits as

    logits = hidden @ model.embed.weight.T  # [B, L, vocab]

so every prediction goes through that table. Everything below calls the
wrapped model, which returns logits. Position i's logits may depend only on
tokens 0..i. Evaluation calls
model(input_ids) in eval mode with no other arguments; that call must stay
within task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP FLOPs per token. Any architecture is allowed
(dense, shared or looped blocks, anything else) within task.WEIGHT_CAP
unique weights.

In training, train.py may pass keyword arguments (say, a number of loops):
model(input_ids, loops=12). How much compute a call costs may depend only on
those arguments, the training/eval mode and the input's shape, never on
random draws inside the model or on the batch size: the harness measures
each kind of call once and charges every later call of that kind its tokens
times that cost.

submission/train.py defines

    def train(ctx: TrainContext) -> None

which trains ctx.model (a metered wrapper around OutputHead(build_model(config)),
see harness.MeteredModel; ctx.model(input_ids) returns logits, and the
submission's own module is ctx.model.inner.body) until the budget runs out: task.TRAIN_TOKENS tokens
through the model and task.TRAIN_FLOPS forward FLOPs, whichever comes first.
A call after that raises BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. The
harness saves the model's weights when train returns or the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable

import torch


class BudgetExhausted(RuntimeError):
    """Raised by ctx.model when it is called after the training budget is spent; ends training normally."""


@dataclass
class Batch:
    tokens: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] input token ids
    targets: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] the next token at each position


@dataclass
class TrainContext:
    model: Any  # harness.MeteredModel
    budget: Any  # harness.Budget: .exhausted, .fraction_used, .used_tokens, .used_flops
    data: Any  # harness.TokenStream: .sample(batch_size, seq_len=task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch
    config: dict  # submission/config.json
    seed: int
    device: torch.device
    log: Callable[[dict], None]  # records a dict of numbers in the run's train.json (and prints it)

When the blocks run out, a new pass starts in a new order.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, data: Path, seed: int, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS):\n        self.tokens = np.memmap(data / \"train.bin\", dtype=np.uint16, mode=\"r\")[:int(corpus)]\n        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n        self.device = torch.device(\"cpu\")\n        self.blocks = (len(self.tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN\n        self.order, self.next_block, self.offset = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0, task.SEQ_LEN\n        self.passes = 0\n\n    def _row_start(self, seq_len: int) -> int:\n        if self.offset + seq_len > task.SEQ_LEN:\n            if self.next_block == self.blocks:\n                self.order, self.next_block = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0\n                self.passes += 1\n            self.block, self.next_block, self.offset = self.order[self.next_block], self.next_block + 1, 0\n        start = int(self.block) * task.SEQ_LEN + self.offset\n        self.offset += seq_len\n        return start\n\n    def sample(self, batch_size: int, seq_len: int = task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch:\n        if not 1 <= seq_len <= task.SEQ_LEN:\n            raise mkdir -p experiments && cp -r submission experiments/baseline && nohup python -u -m lmenv.run train --submission experiments/baseline --out runs/baseline_s0 --seed 0 > runs/baseline_s0.out 2>&1 &
We want to build a language model that only has few fixed weights but may spend plenty of compute on every token. Your goal is to build the best such model you can, through systematic research and experimentation.

## Setup

You get a small transformer language model with about 2.9 million weights (plus its token embedding), trained on web text (FineWeb-Edu) with plain next-token prediction loss. It uses about 12 million FLOPs per token, far less than the cap below allows.

You may change the model and the whole training procedure: the architecture inside the caps below, the losses, the optimiser, the schedule, and so on. The training data, the number of training tokens and the caps are fixed.

## Objective

Minimise the loss (cross-entropy of the next token, in nats) of your trained model on held-out text.

We will retrain your method from scratch once on each of two texts of our own and test each model on held-out text of the similar kind. Your score would the average over both of our held out datasets. Lower loss is better. We will compare your loss with the starting point's and with reference models of our own.

On your dev text, the starting point has a loss of about 3.64 (1.351 bits per byte; see `data/lm/reference.json`).

## The model interface and the caps

`model.py` defines `build_model(config)`. Your model returns hidden states, `hidden = model(input_ids, **options)`, and must keep its token embedding table as `embed` (an `nn.Embedding` of shape (8192, width)). You cannot touch the output layer (it is controlled by the harness): the next-token logits are `hidden @ embed.weight.T`, so every prediction goes through that table. Options you pass in training are up to you and we evaluate with `model(input_ids)` in eval mode. The full interface is documented in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/api.py`.

- Weights: at most 3 million unique weights. Every floating-point parameter and buffer counts once, however often it is used. One table of shape (8192, width) with width at most 512 (the token embedding, or an output head tied to it) does not count.
- Inference compute: at most 120 million FLOPs per token for `model(input_ids)` on a 512-token window, measured with PyTorch's FLOP counter.
- Position i's logits may depend only on tokens 0..i, and evaluation must be deterministic and use no random numbers.

`python -m lmenv.run check` runs these checks.

## Training budget

Training gets 250 million tokens: every token passed through the model in training counts, one pass over the first 250 million tokens of the training text (`ctx.data.sample` returns windows of up to 512 tokens in a shuffled order, each token once). The forward FLOPs of all training calls together may average at most 120 million per token. Backward passes are not counted. The harness measures the cost of each kind of call to the model once and charges every call its tokens and FLOPs. Training stops when either budget is spent.

All computation with trainable weights must happen inside calls to the metered model (`ctx.model`). Losses computed from the logits are free.

## What you have

- `submission/`: the starting point. Whatever is in this folder at the end is your submission.
  - `model.py`: the model.
  - `train.py`: `train(ctx)`, the training loop. It gets the metered model, the budget, the data stream and the config. See `ctx` in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/api.py` and the harness in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py`.
  - `config.json`: passed to both.
- `data/lm/`: dev training and validation text, already tokenized (`train.bin`, `val.bin`, `tokenizer.json`) and `reference.json` (the starting point's dev numbers).
- The `lmenv` package, which runs everything (run from `/workspace`):
  - `python -m lmenv.run check` runs the checks on the model.
  - `python -m lmenv.run train --out runs/NAME --seed N` trains one seed with the same budget and rules we use, then prints the validation loss. `--tokens` sets a smaller budget for quick tests.
  - `python -m lmenv.run eval --run runs/NAME` evaluates a trained run again.
  - `python -m lmenv.run score --runs runs/A runs/B runs/C` averages several runs the way we do and shows how much lower the loss is than the starting point's.
- `timer.sh`: tells you how much time you have left.
- `runs/` for your runs and `snapshots/` for saved versions of your method (see below).

## Running experiments

- One training run of the starting point takes about 2 minutes. Start long jobs in the background, for example `nohup python -m lmenv.run train ... > runs/NAME.out 2>&1 &`, so you can keep working.
- You can run more than one job on the GPU at the same time.
- Results vary between seeds by about a hundredth of a nat. Compare methods over several seeds before trusting a small difference.

## Saving versions of your method

Whenever you have a version you consider an improvement, or before you change direction, save it:

    python -m lmenv.run snapshot --note "what you changed and what you saw" --runs runs/NAME ...

This copies `submission/` into `snapshots/` with your note. We may retrain your snapshots afterwards to see how your method developed. They do not affect your score. Your final score comes from what is in `submission/` at the end.

## Information on the setup

- The machine has one NVIDIA H200 141GB GPU, 16 CPU cores and PyTorch 2.7.1.
- There is no internet access: the commands you run cannot reach the network, and no web search is available.

## Rules

1. There will be no user interaction. You have to operate autonomously.
2. You have a maximum of 4 hours. You can check how much time you have left by running `bash timer.sh`.
3. We retrain your submission with the same harness (`lmenv.harness.train_run`). It must keep the interface of the starting point: `model.py` with `build_model(config)`, `train.py` with `train(ctx)`, and `config.json`.
4. Use the model only through `ctx.model`, and do not work around the budget or the caps (for example, by computing with trainable weights outside the model call, or by making a call's cost depend on the batch size).
5. No distillation and no pretrained weights: the model learns only from the training tokens it is given.
6. Your submission may read only the training tokens it is given and its own files. It must not read the validation text, access the network or other processes, or touch files outside `submission/`.
7. Your submission must run in the current environment (same packages). New packages cannot be installed.
8. You must NOT use any API key or login found in your environment to call an external LLM API directly. They are there to run you (the agent), not for you to query other models.

Submissions that break these rules receive the starting point's score.

Remember: NEVER ask the user for feedback. Just execute actions which make most sense to you. We will retrain and evaluate your submission once you are done.

#14 execcat > experiments/train_advanced.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/train_advanced.py <<'PY'
"""Metered next-token training with AdamW or Muon matrix updates."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


@torch.compile
def orthogonalize(g):
    x = g.bfloat16()
    if x.size(0) > x.size(1):
        x = x.T
    x = x / (x.norm() + 1e-7)
    for _ in range(5):
        a = x @ x.T
        b = -4.7750 * a + 2.0315 * (a @ a)
        x = 3.4445 * x + b @ x
    if g.size(0) > g.size(1):
        x = x.T
    return x


class Muon(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    def __init__(self, params, lr=0.02, momentum=0.95, weight_decay=0.1):
        super().__init__(params, dict(lr=lr, momentum=momentum, weight_decay=weight_decay))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            for p in group['params']:
                if p.grad is None:
                    continue
                g = p.grad
                state = self.state[p]
                if 'momentum' not in state:
                    state['momentum'] = torch.zeros_like(g)
                buf = state['momentum']
                buf.lerp_(g, 1 - group['momentum'])
                g = g.lerp(buf, group['momentum'])
                update = orthogonalize(g)
                scale = max(1.0, p.size(0) / p.size(1)) ** 0.5
                p.mul_(1 - group['lr'] * group['weight_decay'])
                p.add_(update, alpha=-group['lr'] * scale)


@torch.compile
def cross_entropy(logits, targets):
    return F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), targets.flatten())


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config['train']
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    if cfg.get('compile', True) and ctx.device.type == 'cuda':
        model.inner.body.stack = torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params = list(model.parameters())
    use_muon = cfg.get('optimizer', 'adamw') == 'muon'
    if use_muon:
        matrices = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if p.ndim == 2 and 'embed' not in n]
        adam_params = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if p.ndim != 2 or 'embed' in n]
        muon = Muon(matrices, lr=cfg.get('muon_lr', 0.02), weight_decay=cfg['weight_decay'])
    else:
        adam_params = params
    groups = [dict(params=[p for p in adam_params if p.ndim >= 2], weight_decay=cfg['weight_decay']),
              dict(params=[p for p in adam_params if p.ndim < 2], weight_decay=0.0)]
    adam = torch.optim.AdamW(groups, lr=cfg['learning_rate'], betas=(0.9, cfg.get('beta2', 0.95)), fused=ctx.device.type == 'cuda')
    optimizers = [adam, muon] if use_muon else [adam]

    def factor(progress):
        warmup = cfg.get('warmup', 0.05)
        if progress < warmup:
            return max(progress, 1e-4) / warmup
        p = (progress - warmup) / (1 - warmup)
        end = cfg.get('min_lr_ratio', 0.1)
        return end + (1 - end) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, p)))

    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        f = factor(budget.fraction_used)
        for group in adam.param_groups:
            group['lr'] = cfg['learning_rate'] * f
        if use_muon:
            for group in muon.param_groups:
                group['lr'] = cfg.get('muon_lr', 0.02) * f
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
            if budget.exhausted:
                break
            batch = ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = cross_entropy(logits, batch.targets)
            (loss / cfg['accumulation']).backward()
            total += loss.detach()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, cfg.get('grad_clip', 1.0))
        for optimizer in optimizers:
            optimizer.step()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if update % cfg['log_every'] == 0:
            val = total.item() / cfg['accumulation']
            if not math.isfinite(val):
                raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {val} at update {update}')
            ctx.log(dict(update=update, loss=val, lr=adam.param_groups[0]['lr']))

cat > experiments/run_queue.py <<'PY'
import subprocess,time,json,sys
from pathlib import Path
spec=json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text())
wait=spec.get('wait_for',[])
while any(not Path(p).exists() for p in wait):
    time.sleep(5)
active=[]
jobs=iter(spec['jobs'])
left=True
while left or active:
    active=[(p,n) for p,n in active if p.poll() is None]
    while left and len(active)<spec.get('parallel',3):
        j=next(jobs,None)
        if j is None:
            left=False;break
        run=j['run'];name=j['name']
        out=open('runs/'+run+'.out','w')
        cmd=['python','-u','-m','lmenv.run','train','--submission','experiments/'+name,'--out','runs/'+run,'--seed',str(j.get('seed',0)),'--tokens',str(j.get('tokens',250000000))]
        p=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=out,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        out.close();active.append((p,run))
        print('Started',run,p.pid,flush=True)
    time.sleep(5)
print('Queue complete',flush=True)

"""The starting model: a small dense GPT-2-style language model.

Four transformer blocks (width 256, 4 heads, causal attention with RoPE on
queries and keys, GELU-tanh MLP of width 896), each used once per token. The
model returns the final hidden states; the harness turns them into logits
with the token embedding table (tied output head). 2,896,928 unique weights besides
the 8192 x 256 embedding (not counted), about 12M FLOPs per token.

Initialisation: weights N(0, 0.02), biases 0, and every block's output
projections (attention and MLP c_proj) N(0, 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896
    repeats: int = 1
    embed_dim: int = 0
    separate_norms: bool = False
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5


class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * cfg.d_model)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))


class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.c_fc = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.mlp_width)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.c_proj(F.gelu(self.c_fc(x), approximate="tanh"))


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln_1 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)

    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))


def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)


class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        ed = cfg.embed_dim or cfg.d_model
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, ed)
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(ed, cfg.d_model, bias=False) if ed != cfg.d_model else nn.Identity()
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, ed, bias=False) if ed != cfg.d_model else nn.Identity()
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(ed, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.step_norms = nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model), nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model)]) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats)) if cfg.separate_norms else None
        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                if module.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats))
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version

    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        for step in range(self.cfg.repeats * self.cfg.n_layers):
            block = self.blocks[step % self.cfg.n_layers]
            if self.step_norms is None:
                h = block(h, cos, sin)
            else:
                norms = self.step_norms[step]
                h = h + block.attn(norms[0](h), cos, sin)
                h = h + block.mlp(norms[1](h))
        return h

    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.in_proj(self.embed(input_ids)), angles.cos(), angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))  # the harness applies the tied output head


def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""

    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))

#25 execcat > experiments/model_flexible.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/model_flexible.py <<'PY'
"""Causal transformers with independently shared attention and MLP modules."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 512
    embed_dim: int = 512
    attn_dim: int = 512
    n_heads: int = 8
    n_attn: int = 1
    n_mlp: int = 1
    mlp_width: int = 1792
    depth: int = 8
    norm: str = 'layer'
    activation: str = 'gelu'
    input_norm: bool = False
    qk_norm: bool = False
    bias: bool = True
    residual_gates: bool = False
    rope_theta: float = 10000.0

class RMSNorm(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, width):
        super().__init__()
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(width))
    def forward(self, x):
        return F.rms_norm(x, (x.size(-1),), self.weight, 1e-5)

def norm(width, kind):
    return RMSNorm(width) if kind == 'rms' else nn.LayerNorm(width, eps=1e-5)

def rotate(x):
    a,b=x.chunk(2,dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-b,a),dim=-1)

class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg=cfg
        self.qkv=nn.Linear(cfg.d_model,3*cfg.attn_dim,bias=cfg.bias)
        self.out=nn.Linear(cfg.attn_dim,cfg.d_model,bias=cfg.bias)
    def forward(self,x,cos,sin):
        b,l,_=x.shape
        c=self.cfg
        q,k,v=self.qkv(x).chunk(3,dim=-1)
        q,k,v=[t.view(b,l,c.n_heads,c.attn_dim//c.n_heads).transpose(1,2) for t in (q,k,v)]
        if c.qk_norm:
            q=F.rms_norm(q,(q.size(-1),),eps=1e-6)
            k=F.rms_norm(k,(k.size(-1),),eps=1e-6)
        q=q*cos+rotate(q)*sin
        k=k*cos+rotate(k)*sin
        h=F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,is_causal=True)
        return self.out(h.transpose(1,2).reshape(b,l,c.attn_dim))

class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,c):
        super().__init__()
        self.activation=c.activation
        self.up=nn.Linear(c.d_model,c.mlp_width*(2 if c.activation=='swiglu' else 1),bias=c.bias)
        self.down=nn.Linear(c.mlp_width,c.d_model,bias=c.bias)
    def forward(self,x):
        x=self.up(x)
        if self.activation=='swiglu':
            a,b=x.chunk(2,dim=-1);x=F.silu(a)*b
        elif self.activation=='relu2':
            x=F.relu(x).square()
        else:
            x=F.gelu(x,approximate='tanh')
        return self.down(x)

class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,c):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg=c
        self.embed=nn.Embedding(c.vocab_size,c.embed_dim)
        self.in_proj=nn.Linear(c.embed_dim,c.d_model,bias=False) if c.embed_dim!=c.d_model else nn.Identity()
        self.out_proj=nn.Linear(c.d_model,c.embed_dim,bias=False) if c.embed_dim!=c.d_model else nn.Identity()
        self.attns=nn.ModuleList(Attention(c) for _ in range(c.n_attn))
        self.mlps=nn.ModuleList(MLP(c) for _ in range(c.n_mlp))
        self.norms=nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([norm(c.d_model,c.norm),norm(c.d_model,c.norm)]) for _ in range(c.depth))
        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)
        if c.residual_gates:
            self.residual=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(c.depth,2,c.d_model))
            self.injection=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(c.depth,c.d_model).unsqueeze(1))
        hd=c.attn_dim//c.n_heads
        self.register_buffer('inv_freq',1.0/c.rope_theta**(torch.arange(0,hd,2,dtype=torch.float32)/hd),persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight,std=0.02)
        for m in self.modules():
            if isinstance(m,nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(m.weight,std=0.02)
                if m.bias is not None: nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)
        for a in self.attns: nn.init.normal_(a.out.weight,std=0.02/math.sqrt(2*c.depth))
        for m in self.mlps: nn.init.normal_(m.down.weight,std=0.02/math.sqrt(2*c.depth))
        self.stack=self.apply_blocks
    def apply_blocks(self,h,cos,sin):
        initial=h
        for i in range(self.cfg.depth):
            a=self.attns[i%self.cfg.n_attn](self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin)
            h=h+a*(self.residual[i,0] if self.cfg.residual_gates else 1)
            m=self.mlps[i%self.cfg.n_mlp](self.norms[i][1](h))
            h=h+m*(self.residual[i,1] if self.cfg.residual_gates else 1)
            if self.cfg.residual_gates: h=h+self.injection[i]*initial
        return h
    def forward(self,input_ids):
        p=torch.arange(input_ids.size(1),device=input_ids.device,dtype=torch.float32)
        angles=torch.outer(p,self.inv_freq)
        angles=torch.cat((angles,angles),dim=-1)[None,None]
        h=self.in_proj(self.embed(input_ids))
        if self.cfg.input_norm: h=F.rms_norm(h,(h.size(-1),),eps=1e-5)
        h=self.stack(h,angles.cos(),angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))

def build_model(config):
    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config['vocab_size'],**config.get('model',{})))

#37 execcat > experiments/model_memory.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/model_memory.py <<'PY'
"""Repeated causal transformer with an embedding-tied feed-forward memory."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn

class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,w):
        super().__init__()
        self.qkv=nn.Linear(w,3*w,bias=False)
        self.out=nn.Linear(w,w,bias=False)
    def forward(self,x,cos,sin):
        b,l,w=x.shape
        q,k,v=[t.view(b,l,8,w//8).transpose(1,2) for t in self.qkv(x).chunk(3,-1)]
        def rot(t):
            a,b=t.chunk(2,-1);return torch.cat((-b,a),-1)
        q=q*cos+rot(q)*sin;k=k*cos+rot(k)*sin
        a=F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,is_causal=True)
        return self.out(a.transpose(1,2).reshape(b,l,w))

class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,c):
        super().__init__()
        self.depth=c.get('depth',5);self.act=c.get('activation','gelu')
        self.embed=nn.Embedding(8192,512)
        self.attns=nn.ModuleList([Attention(512),Attention(512)])
        self.up=nn.Linear(512,512,bias=False)
        self.down=nn.Linear(512,512,bias=False)
        self.norms=nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([nn.LayerNorm(512),nn.LayerNorm(512)]) for _ in range(self.depth))
        self.ln_f=nn.LayerNorm(512)
        self.memory_scale=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.depth))
        self.register_buffer('inv_freq',1.0/10000**(torch.arange(0,64,2).float()/64),persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight,std=0.02)
        for m in self.modules():
            if isinstance(m,nn.Linear): nn.init.normal_(m.weight,std=0.02)
        for a in self.attns: nn.init.normal_(a.out.weight,std=0.02/math.sqrt(2*self.depth))
        nn.init.normal_(self.down.weight,std=0.02/math.sqrt(2*self.depth))
        self.stack=self.apply_blocks
    def apply_blocks(self,h,cos,sin):
        for i in range(self.depth):
            h=h+self.attns[i%2](self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin)
            q=self.up(self.norms[i][1](h))
            a=F.linear(q,self.embed.weight)
            if self.act=='softmax': a=a.softmax(-1)*8
            else: a=F.gelu(a,approximate='tanh')/8
            v=a@self.embed.weight
            h=h+self.down(v)*self.memory_scale[i]
        return h
    def forward(self,input_ids):
        p=torch.arange(input_ids.size(1),device=input_ids.device,dtype=torch.float32)
        a=torch.outer(p,self.inv_freq);a=torch.cat((a,a),-1)[None,None]
        return self.ln_f(self.stack(self.embed(input_ids),a.cos(),a.sin()))

def build_model(config):
    return GPT(config.get('model',{}))

#61 execcat > experiments/NOTES.md <<'EOF'
cat > experiments/NOTES.md <<'EOF'
# Research log

All experiments use the supplied shuffled one-pass training stream, next-token targets, and the metered model. No external data or weights are used. The validation set is evaluated only by the provided harness. Every architecture keeps the tied 8192-token output embedding.

## Initial controlled experiments

- Reproduced the starter with 250M tokens, seed 0: 3.6396 nats (published reference mean 3.6438).
- Compared recurrent stacks at state widths 256, 384, and 512, using the full exempt 512-wide embedding, step-specific layer norms, and approximately 2.9M counted weights.
- Introduced Muon for non-embedding matrices, keeping AdamW for embeddings and scalar/vector parameters. Compiled the cross-entropy loss and used batch 64 in one microbatch to accelerate the new training loop.
- A critical negative finding: the baseline Muon short-run improvement (4.0318 -> 3.8720 at 50M tokens) did not survive a full run (3.6528 versus AdamW 3.6396). Thus short runs screen hypotheses but do not determine the final method.
- The width-512 shared block, repeated eight times, improved full-budget loss to 3.5753 with Muon and 3.5562 with AdamW. Saved as snapshots 002 and 003.
- Repeating the original shared block twelve times did not improve the 50M-token Muon result (3.8527 vs 3.8524).
- Separately sharing attention and feed-forward modules, or compressing attention width, produced similar or worse 50M-token results.
- RMS normalization, normalized queries/keys, and normalized input embeddings improved the 50M-token result to 3.7874. Replacing GELU with SwiGLU improved this to 3.7771. Full-budget comparisons of both optimizers are scheduled.

## Remaining experiments

- Greater depth with the improved normalization: 12 and 16 steps. The latter uses 118.49M FLOPs/token and 2.933M counted weights.
- Training recipe: matrix learning rate, matrix weight decay, batch size, embedding learning rate, and final cooldown schedule.
- Step embeddings, low-rank step adapters, residual/input gates, intermediate next-token supervision, nonlinear output projection, and an embedding-tied feed-forward memory.
- Full-budget repeats of the eventual leaders across multiple random seeds.

Run `python experiments/results.py` for completed results and resource counts. The authoritative per-run training and evaluation records are in `runs/`.
EOF

#67 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import shutil,json,subprocess
base_model=Path('experiments/m512_swiglu/model.py').read_text()
base_cfg=json.load(open('experiments/m512_swiglu/config.json'))
variants={}
s=base_model.replace('        self.qkv=nn.Linear', '        self.qk_gain=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(cfg.n_heads))\n        self.qkv=nn.Linear')
s=s.replace('        q=q*cos+rotate(q)*sin', '        q=q*self.qk_gain[None,:,None,None]\n        q=q*cos+rotate(q)*sin')
variants['swiglu_temp']=s
s=base_model.replace('        self.qkv=nn.Linear', '        self.value_gain=nn.Parameter(torch.full((cfg.n_heads,),0.1))\n        self.qkv=nn.Linear')
s=s.replace('def forward(self,x,cos,sin):','def forward(self,x,cos,sin,initial):')
s=s.replace('        if c.qk_norm:', '        v=v+self.value_gain[None,:,None,None]*initial.view(b,l,c.n_heads,c.attn_dim//c.n_heads).transpose(1,2)\n        if c.qk_norm:')
s=s.replace('(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin)', '(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,initial)')
variants['swiglu_value']=s
s=base_model.replace('        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)', '        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.output_skip=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(c.embed_dim))')
s=s.replace('        h=self.stack(h,angles.cos(),angles.sin())\n        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))', '        initial=h\n        h=self.stack(h,angles.cos(),angles.sin())\n        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))+initial*self.output_skip')
variants['swiglu_outskip']=s
s=base_model.replace('        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)', '        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.mix_logits=nn.Parameter(torch.tensor([-3.0]*(c.depth-1)+[0.0]))')
s=s.replace('        initial=h\n        for i', '        initial=h\n        outputs=[]\n        for i')
s=s.replace('        return h\n    def forward', '            outputs.append(self.ln_f(h))\n        mix=self.mix_logits.softmax(0)\n        return sum(mix[i]*outputs[i] for i in range(self.cfg.depth))\n    def forward')
s=s.replace('return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))', 'return h')
variants['swiglu_mix']=s
for name,s in variants.items():
 p=Path('experiments')/name;p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 (p/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy('experiments/train_advanced.py',p/'train.py')
 (p/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(base_cfg,indent=2))
spec=dict(parallel=2,wait_for=['runs/m512_gates_50m_s0/eval.json'],jobs=[dict(name=n,run=n+'_50m_s0',tokens=50000000) for n in variants])
Path('experiments/queue7.json').write_text(json.dumps(spec))
p=subprocess.Popen(['python','-u','experiments/run_queue.py','experiments/queue7.json'],stdout=open('runs/queue7.out','w'),stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
print(p.pid)

from pathlib import Path
import shutil,json
s=Path('experiments/m512_swiglu/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('def forward(self,x,cos,sin):', 'def forward(self,x,cos,sin,adapter):')
s=s.replace('q,k,v=self.qkv(x).chunk(3,dim=-1)', 'q,k,v=(self.qkv(x)+adapter(x)).chunk(3,dim=-1)')
s=s.replace('def forward(self,x):\n        x=self.up(x)', 'def forward(self,x,adapter):\n        x=self.up(x)+adapter(x)')
s=s.replace('        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)', '        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.q_adapters=nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(c.d_model,8,bias=False),nn.Linear(8,3*c.attn_dim,bias=False)) for _ in range(c.depth))\n        self.m_adapters=nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(c.d_model,16,bias=False),nn.Linear(16,2*c.mlp_width,bias=False)) for _ in range(c.depth))')
s=s.replace('        self.stack=self.apply_blocks', '        for a in self.q_adapters: nn.init.zeros_(a[1].weight)\n        for a in self.m_adapters: nn.init.zeros_(a[1].weight)\n        self.stack=self.apply_blocks')
s=s.replace('(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin)', '(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,self.q_adapters[i])')
s=s.replace('(self.norms[i][1](h))', '(self.norms[i][1](h),self.m_adapters[i])')
cfg=json.load(open('experiments/m512_swiglu_adam/config.json'));cfg['model']['mlp_width']=960
p=Path('experiments/swi_adapters');p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(p/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy('experiments/train_advanced.py',p/'train.py')
(p/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(cfg,indent=2))

#88 execcat > experiments/run_dynamic.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/run_dynamic.py <<'PY'
import json,subprocess,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
path=Path(sys.argv[1])
spec=json.loads(path.read_text())
while any(not Path(p).exists() for p in spec.get('wait_for',[])):
    time.sleep(5)
started=set()
active=[]
while True:
    spec=json.loads(path.read_text())
    active=[(p,n) for p,n in active if p.poll() is None]
    for j in spec['jobs']:
        if len(active)>=spec.get('parallel',4): break
        run=j['run']
        if run in started: continue
        started.add(run)
        if Path('runs',run,'eval.json').exists(): continue
        out=open('runs/'+run+'.out','w')
        cmd=['python','-u','-m','lmenv.run','train','--submission','experiments/'+j['name'],'--out','runs/'+run,'--seed',str(j.get('seed',0)),'--tokens',str(j.get('tokens',250000000))]
        p=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=out,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        out.close();active.append((p,run))
        print('Started',run,p.pid,flush=True)
    Path('runs/dynamic_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(active=[dict(pid=p.pid,run=n) for p,n in active],started=sorted(started)),indent=2))
    if not active and spec.get('exit_when_done',False) and all(j['run'] in started for j in spec['jobs']): break
    time.sleep(5)
PY

cat > experiments/STATUS.md <<'EOF'
# Current research state

Working directory /workspace. User requests autonomous research and final submission under /workspace/submission, no questions, max 4h (timer.sh). No subagents permitted by developer. All model/training code is self-contained and metered; only agent-side orchestration accesses experiments/runs. No external data/network.

## Best saved method

submission/ currently equals experiments/m512_swiglu_adam: width512 shared single block, 8 steps, per-step RMSNorm, normalized embeddings and Q/K, SwiGLU width1216, no biases, AdamW 0.002 cosine to10%, batch64. 2,925,088 counted weights, 63,438,848 FLOPs/token. Seed0 full250M loss3.5329; same model Muon seed0 3.5414. Snapshot004. Previous best snapshot003 shared GELU/LN width512,8steps Adam loss3.5562. Baseline reproduced3.6396; reference3.643780.

## Experiment management

All experiment folders contain independent model.py/train.py/config.json. Training runs never use a source folder that gets edited while they run. Results in runs/NAME/eval.json and train.json. Logs runs/NAME.out. `python experiments/results.py` prints results. Full-budget defaults250M; *_50m_* are screening proxies, NOT comparable to full losses.

Original queues1-6 have finished or are about to finish. Queue7 (four SwiGLU feature variants) and queue9 (adapters + low-LR Adam) are the remaining short-run queues. Queue8 was replaced; its controller was killed before starting any job.

NEW unified dynamic controller PID14405 runs experiments/run_dynamic.py experiments/queue_dynamic.json, log runs/dynamic.out. It waits for all current jobs to finish, then keeps up to4 jobs running. It rereads its JSON before every launch: you can append or reorder unstarted jobs, change parallelism, or set exit_when_done. It skips already evaluated run names. Runtime status in runs/dynamic_status.json, listing active child PIDs and names and all started jobs. It deliberately stays alive when idle unless exit_when_done=true. Stop it at final completion.

Dynamic queue includes full seed1 replications of m512_swiglu_adam and m512_swiglu (Muon), followed by architecture/depth and training-recipe50M proxies. Architecture grid uses state width512 throughout, different unique block counts and attention/FF intermediate dimensions, matching parameters and compute: one block8steps, two blocks16steps, four blocks32steps all ~63.4MFLOPs. Oneblock16steps or twoblocks32steps ~118.49MFLOPs. Also shallower controls, step embeddings, and training LR/batch/decay variants.

## Findings

Short-run optimizer rankings differ from full-budget rankings. Baseline Muon50M3.872 versus Adam50M4.032, but full Muon3.653 versus Adam3.640. Must choose final using full-budget runs and repeated seeds. Looped narrower state widths256/384 improved baseline but lost to width512 at full budget. Initial attempts at shared embedding feedforward memory, lowrank adapters, intermediate loss, and simple nonlinear output projections did not clearly help50M. Modern normalization/SwiGLU is the strongest architecture change so far. Lowest AdamLR0.001 proxy learned too slowly (4.096).

Next: inspect current and queued results, promote promising depth or recipe variants to250M, verify gains over2-3seeds, snapshot improvements, clean final submission only after choice, run final cap/static/causality/determinism checks and score selected runs. Keep user informed with concise commentary at least each60s. No questions.

from pathlib import Path
import shutil,json
s=Path('experiments/m512_swiglu/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)', '        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.future_norm=norm(c.d_model,c.norm)\n        self.future_out=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.future_shift=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(c.d_model))')
s=s.replace('        return h\n    def forward(self,input_ids):', '        if self.training:\n            future=h+self.mlps[0](self.future_norm(h)+self.future_shift)\n            return h,future\n        return h\n    def forward(self,input_ids):')
s=s.replace('        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))','        if self.training:\n            h,future=h\n            return torch.cat((self.ln_f(h),self.future_out(future)),dim=1)\n        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))')

 p=Path('experiments')/name;p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 (p/'model.py').write_text(s)
 t=Path('experiments/train_advanced.py').read_text()
 coefficient='0.3 * max(0.0, 1.0 - budget.fraction_used)' if decay else '0.3'
 t=t.replace('loss = cross_entropy(logits, batch.targets)',f"length = batch.tokens.size(1)\n            primary = cross_entropy(logits[:, :length], batch.targets)\n            auxiliary = cross_entropy(logits[:, length:-1], batch.targets[:, 1:])\n            loss = primary + ({coefficient}) * auxiliary")
 (p/'train.py').write_text(t);(p/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(base,indent=2))
q=Path('experiments/queue_dynamic.json');cfg=json.loads(q.read_text())

#104 execcat > experiments/soap_class.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/soap_class.py <<'PY'
class SOAP(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    """Adam moments in periodically updated gradient-covariance eigenbases."""
    def __init__(self, params, lr=0.002, weight_decay=0.1, frequency=32):
        super().__init__(params,dict(lr=lr,weight_decay=weight_decay,frequency=frequency))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            for p in group['params']:
                if p.grad is None: continue
                g=p.grad.float()
                st=self.state[p]
                if not st:
                    st['step']=0
                    st['m']=torch.zeros_like(g)
                    st['v']=torch.zeros_like(g)
                    st['left']=torch.zeros(g.size(0),g.size(0),device=g.device) if g.size(0)<=768 else None
                    st['right']=torch.zeros(g.size(1),g.size(1),device=g.device) if g.size(1)<=768 else None
                    st['ql']=None;st['qr']=None
                st['step']+=1
                step=st['step']
                if st['left'] is not None: st['left'].lerp_(g@g.T,0.05)
                if st['right'] is not None: st['right'].lerp_(g.T@g,0.05)
                if (step-1)%group['frequency']==0:
                    if st['left'] is not None:
                        q=torch.linalg.eigh(st['left'])[1]
                        if st['ql'] is not None:
                            rot=q.T@st['ql']
                            st['v']=rot.square()@st['v']
                        st['ql']=q
                    if st['right'] is not None:
                        q=torch.linalg.eigh(st['right'])[1]
                        if st['qr'] is not None:
                            rot=st['qr'].T@q
                            st['v']=st['v']@rot.square()
                        st['qr']=q
                gp=g
                if st['ql'] is not None: gp=st['ql'].T@gp
                if st['qr'] is not None: gp=gp@st['qr']
                st['m'].lerp_(g,0.1)
                st['v'].lerp_(gp.square(),0.05)
                mp=st['m']
                if st['ql'] is not None: mp=st['ql'].T@mp
                if st['qr'] is not None: mp=mp@st['qr']
                upd=mp/(st['v'].sqrt()+1e-8)
                if st['ql'] is not None: upd=st['ql']@upd
                if st['qr'] is not None: upd=upd@st['qr'].T
                step_size=group['lr']*(1-0.95**step)**0.5/(1-0.9**step)
                p.mul_(1-group['lr']*group['weight_decay'])
                p.add_(upd,alpha=-step_size)
PY

from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/m512_swiglu/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('        self.qkv=nn.Linear', '        self.qk_gain=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(cfg.n_heads))\n        self.value_gain=nn.Parameter(torch.full((cfg.n_heads,),0.1))\n        self.qkv=nn.Linear')
s=s.replace('def forward(self,x,cos,sin):','def forward(self,x,cos,sin,value):')
s=s.replace('        if c.qk_norm:', '        v=v+self.value_gain[None,:,None,None]*value.view(b,l,c.n_heads,c.attn_dim//c.n_heads).transpose(1,2)\n        if c.qk_norm:')
s=s.replace('        q=q*cos+rotate(q)*sin', '        q=q*self.qk_gain[None,:,None,None]\n        q=q*cos+rotate(q)*sin')
s=s.replace('        initial=h\n        for i', '        initial=h\n        values=[F.linear(initial,a.qkv.weight[-self.cfg.attn_dim:]) for a in self.attns]\n        for i')
s=s.replace('(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin)', '(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,values[i%self.cfg.n_attn])')
p=Path('experiments/two32_tv');p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

I’ve added two small tests of how the model preserves token information across repeated steps: one gives steps separate attention gains; the other adds learned residual scales and a path that reintroduces the input representation. Both add few weights and keep the same main matrix operations.

Ran 1 commandexec ×1
#148 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/swi_tv_adam/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)', '        self.ln_f=norm(c.embed_dim,c.norm)\n        self.res_scales=nn.Parameter(torch.ones(c.depth*2*c.d_model))\n        self.input_scales=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(c.depth*c.d_model))')
s=s.replace('h=h+a*(self.residual[i,0] if self.cfg.residual_gates else 1)', 'h=h+a*self.res_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,2,self.cfg.d_model)[i,0]')
s=s.replace('h=h+m*(self.residual[i,1] if self.cfg.residual_gates else 1)', 'h=h+m*self.res_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,2,self.cfg.d_model)[i,1]+self.input_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,self.cfg.d_model)[i]*initial')
p=Path('experiments/tv_gated');p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(p/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy('experiments/train_advanced.py',p/'train.py');shutil.copy('experiments/swi_tv_adam/config.json',p/'config.json')
s=Path('experiments/two32_tv/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('values=[F.linear(initial,a.qkv.weight[-self.cfg.attn_dim:]) for a in self.attns]', 'values=list(initial.split(self.cfg.attn_dim,dim=-1))')
p=Path('experiments/two32_raw');p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(p/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy('experiments/train_advanced.py',p/'train.py');shutil.copy('experiments/two32_tv/config.json',p/'config.json')
q=Path('experiments/queue_dynamic.json');c=json.loads(q.read_text())
c['jobs'].insert(6,dict(name='tv_gated',run='tv_gated_50m_s0',tokens=50000000))
c['jobs'].append(dict(name='two32_raw',run='two32_raw_50m_s0',tokens=50000000))

from pathlib import Path
import json
p=Path('experiments/queue_dynamic.json');s=json.loads(p.read_text());started=set(json.load(open('runs/dynamic_status.json'))['started'])
priority=['tv_stepgain','tv_gated','swi_partial','swi_ropeheads','swi_mtp','swi_mtp_const','swi_wide8','swi_wide16','swi_four32']
ranks={n:i for i,n in enumerate(priority)}
full=[j for j in s['jobs'] if j.get('tokens',250000000)==250000000 and j['run'] not in started]
active=[j for j in s['jobs'] if j['run'] in started]
short=[j for j in s['jobs'] if j.get('tokens',250000000)!=250000000 and j['run'] not in started]
short.sort(key=lambda j:ranks.get(j['name'],len(priority)))
s['jobs']=active+full+short;p.write_text(json.dumps(s,indent=2))

#171 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/tv_gated/model.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('torch.ones(cfg.n_heads)', 'torch.ones(cfg.depth*cfg.n_heads)')
s=s.replace('torch.full((cfg.n_heads,),0.1)', 'torch.full((cfg.depth*cfg.n_heads,),0.1)')
s=s.replace('def forward(self,x,cos,sin,initial):', 'def forward(self,x,cos,sin,initial,step):')
s=s.replace('self.value_gain[None,:,None,None]', 'self.value_gain.view(c.depth,c.n_heads)[step][None,:,None,None]')
s=s.replace('self.qk_gain[None,:,None,None]', 'self.qk_gain.view(c.depth,c.n_heads)[step][None,:,None,None]')
s=s.replace('(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,initial)', '(self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,initial,i)')
p=Path('experiments/tv_d16_maxgated');p.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(p/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy('experiments/swi_soap/train.py',p/'train.py')
shutil.copy('experiments/tv_d16_soap_max/config.json',p/'config.json')
q=Path('experiments/queue_dynamic.json');c=json.loads(q.read_text())
c['jobs'].insert(0,dict(name='tv_d16_maxgated',run='tv_d16_maxgated_s0'))
q.write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))

#179 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
source=Path('experiments/swi_soap/train.py').read_text()
soap=source[source.index('class SOAP'):source.index('@torch.compile\ndef cross_entropy')]
prefix='''"""Single-pass next-token training with gradient-preconditioned matrix updates.

Embeddings and normalization/gain parameters use AdamW. Other matrices use
Adam moments in periodically updated gradient-covariance eigenbases.
All network computation is through the harness's metered model.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

'''
train='''@torch.compile
def cross_entropy(logits, targets):
    return F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), targets.flatten())


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config['train']
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    if cfg.get('compile', True) and ctx.device.type == 'cuda':
        model.inner.body.stack = torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params = list(model.parameters())
    matrices = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if p.ndim == 2 and 'embed' not in n]
    adam_params = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if p.ndim != 2 or 'embed' in n]
    matrix_lr = cfg.get('matrix_learning_rate', cfg.get('muon_lr', 0.002))
    matrix_optimizer = SOAP(matrices, lr=matrix_lr, weight_decay=cfg['weight_decay'])
    adam = torch.optim.AdamW([
        dict(params=[p for p in adam_params if p.ndim >= 2], weight_decay=cfg['weight_decay']),
        dict(params=[p for p in adam_params if p.ndim < 2], weight_decay=0.0),
    ], lr=cfg['learning_rate'], betas=(0.9, cfg.get('beta2', 0.95)), fused=ctx.device.type == 'cuda')

    def factor(progress):
        warmup = cfg.get('warmup', 0.05)
        if progress < warmup:
            return max(progress, 1e-4) / warmup
        p = (progress - warmup) / (1 - warmup)
        end = cfg.get('min_lr_ratio', 0.0)
        return end + (1 - end) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, p)))

    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        f = factor(budget.fraction_used)
        for group in adam.param_groups:
            group['lr'] = cfg['learning_rate'] * f
        for group in matrix_optimizer.param_groups:
            group['lr'] = matrix_lr * f
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
            if budget.exhausted:
                break
            batch = ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = cross_entropy(logits, batch.targets)
            (loss / cfg['accumulation']).backward()
            total += loss.detach()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, cfg.get('grad_clip', 1.0))
        for optimizer in (adam, matrix_optimizer):
            optimizer.step()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if update % cfg['log_every'] == 0:
            value = total.item() / cfg['accumulation']
            if not math.isfinite(value):
                raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {value} at update {update}')
            ctx.log(dict(update=update, loss=value, lr=adam.param_groups[0]['lr']))
'''
Path('experiments/final_template_train.py').write_text(prefix+soap+train)
PY

#199 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
p=Path('experiments/queue_dynamic.json');s=json.loads(p.read_text());started=set(json.load(open('runs/dynamic_status.json'))['started'])
new=dict(name='two32_tv',run='two32_tv_50m_s0',tokens=50000000)
if not any(j['run']==new['run'] for j in s['jobs']):s['jobs'].append(new)
a=[j for j in s['jobs'] if j['run'] in started or j.get('tokens',250000000)==250000000]
b=[j for j in s['jobs'] if j['run'] not in started and j.get('tokens',250000000)!=250000000]
ranks={'two32_raw':0,'two32_tv':1}
b.sort(key=lambda j:ranks.get(j['name'],2))
s['jobs']=a+b;p.write_text(json.dumps(s,indent=2))

self.stack=self.apply_blocks\n    def apply_blocks(self,h,cos,sin):\n        initial=h\n        for i in range(self.cfg.depth):\n            a=self.attns[i%self.cfg.n_attn](self.norms[i][0](h),cos,sin,initial,i)\n            h=h+a*self.res_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,2,self.cfg.d_model)[i,0]\n            m=self.mlps[i%self.cfg.n_mlp](self.norms[i][1](h))\n            h=h+m*self.res_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,2,self.cfg.d_model)[i,1]+self.input_scales.view(self.cfg.depth,self.cfg.d_model)[i]*initial\n            if self.cfg.residual_gates: h=h+self.injection[i]*initial\n        return h\n    def forward(self,input_ids):\n        p=torch.arange(input_ids.size(1),device=input_ids.device,dtype=torch.float32)\n        angles=torch.outer(p,self.inv_freq)\n        angles=torch.cat((angles,angles),dim=-1)[None,None]\n        h=self.in_proj(self.embed(input_ids))\n        if self.cfg.input_norm: h=F.rms_norm(h,(h.size(-1),),eps=1e-5)\n        h=self.stack(h,angles.cos(),angles.sin())\n        return self.ln_f(self.out_proj(h))\n\ndef build_model(config):\n    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config['vocab_size'],**config.get('model',{})))\n"}}

The learned step-specific gains vary substantially across the 16 passes. Later passes use more of the original token representation in attention, while the direct input contribution changes sign. That suggests these small parameters give the shared block useful specialization. I’m keeping them in the optimizer comparisons.

#230 execsed -n '334,405p' /opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py
sed -n '334,405p' /opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py
sed -n '1,120p' /opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/checks.py
def train_run(submission: Path, data: Path, out: Path, seed: int, device, tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS,
              log=print, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS) -> dict:
    """Train one seed of the submission; writes OUT/model.pt and OUT/train.json and returns the record.

    ``tokens`` below TRAIN_TOKENS scales both budgets down (a quick proxy run).
    ``corpus`` (design experiments only) sets how many training tokens are sampled from.
    """

    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
    record: dict = {"seed": seed, "status": "error", "error": None, "budget_tokens": tokens, "corpus": corpus,
                    "history": []}

    def finish(**fields) -> dict:
        record.update(fields)
        (out / "train.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
        return record

    try:
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed)
        model = build(submission, vocab, device)
        trainer = load_module(submission, "train")
        if trainer is None:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/train.py is missing")
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"building the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    failures = interface_failures(model, vocab, device)
    if failures:
        return finish(error=failures[0])
    try:
        caps, failures = cap_failures(model, vocab, device)
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"measuring the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    log(f"{caps['weights']['counted']:,} unique weights (+{caps['weights']['exempt']:,} embedding), "
        f"{caps['inference_flops_per_token'] / 1e6:.1f}M inference FLOPs per token")
    if failures:
        return finish(error="over the caps: " + "; ".join(failures), caps=caps)
    meter = Budget(tokens=tokens, flops=tokens * task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP)
    metered = MeteredModel(model, meter)
    stream = TokenStream(data, seed, corpus)
    stream.device = device
    history = record["history"]

    def record_log(entry: dict) -> None:
        entry = {**entry, "tokens": meter.used_tokens, "flops": meter.used_flops, "elapsed_s": round(meter.elapsed_s, 1)}
        history.append(entry)
        log("  ".join(f"{k} {v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"{k} {v}" for k, v in entry.items()))

    ctx = TrainContext(model=metered, budget=meter, data=stream, config=read_config(submission), seed=seed,
                       device=device, log=record_log)
    status, error = "complete", None
    started = time.time()
    try:
        trainer.train(ctx)
    except BudgetExhausted:
        pass
    except Exception as e:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        status, error = "error", f"train.py: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]}"
    torch.save(saved_state(model), out / "model.pt")
    return finish(status=status, error=error, caps=caps, used_tokens=meter.used_tokens, used_flops=meter.used_flops,
                  call_costs=metered.cost_table(), train_seconds=time.time() - started,
                  corpus_passes=stream.passes + stream.next_block / stream.blocks)


_TOKEN_BYTES: dict = {}


def token_bytes(tokenizer: Path, eot: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """UTF-8 bytes of each token of our byte-level BPE (the end-of-text token counts as one byte)."""

    key = str(tokenizer)
"""Checks the grader runs on a submission, outside the training process.

* weights      unique floating-point weights against the cap, one embedding
               table exempt (harness.unique_weights)
* flops        inference FLOPs per token of model(ids) in eval mode, on random
               and on real text, against the cap
* interface    the model has an embed table, and with the harness's output
               layer (harness.OutputHead) is causal and gives [B, L, vocab] logits
               (harness.interface_failures)
* randomness   evaluation draws no random numbers (no weights generated from
               a seed inside forward) and two passes give identical logits
* call costs   every kind of training call recorded in train.json costs the
               same per token at batch size 1 and 2 and under two seeds, so
               the harness's once-per-kind measurement is honest
* state        persistent buffers and frozen parameters, listed for review
* static scan  source patterns that break the rules (network, subprocess,
               custom kernels, writing to the harness's counters, calling the
               unwrapped model in training) or that need a closer look
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils._python_dispatch import TorchDispatchMode

from lmenv import task
from lmenv.harness import cap_failures, flops_per_token, interface_failures

RANDOM_OPS = {"rand", "randn", "randint", "randperm", "normal", "normal_", "uniform_", "bernoulli", "bernoulli_",
              "multinomial", "exponential_", "random_", "native_dropout", "rand_like", "randn_like", "randint_like"}


class RandomOps(TorchDispatchMode):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.seen: set[str] = set()

    def __torch_dispatch__(self, func, types, args=(), kwargs=None):
        name = func.overloadpacket.__name__
        if name in RANDOM_OPS:
            self.seen.add(name)
        return func(*args, **(kwargs or {}))


@dataclass
class CheckReport:
    weights: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    inference_flops_per_token: float = 0.0
    inference_flops_on_text: float = 0.0
    random_ops_in_eval: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    deterministic: bool = True
    call_costs: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    static_findings: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    state_notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    failures: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def passed(self) -> bool:
        return not self.failures

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {**asdict(self), "passed": self.passed}


@torch.no_grad()
def check_eval(model, ids: torch.Tensor, text: torch.Tensor | None, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """FLOPs on real text, random draws and determinism of model(ids) in eval mode."""

    if text is not None:
        report.inference_flops_on_text = flops_per_token(model, text)
        if report.inference_flops_on_text > task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP:
            report.failures.append(f"inference costs {report.inference_flops_on_text / 1e6:.1f}M FLOPs per token on "
                                   f"real text (cap {task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP / 1e6:.0f}M)")
    model.eval()
    mode = RandomOps()
    with mode:
        first = model(ids)
    report.random_ops_in_eval = sorted(mode.seen)
    if mode.seen:
        report.failures.append("evaluation draws random numbers (" + ", ".join(sorted(mode.seen)) + "); "
                               "model(ids) in eval mode must be deterministic")
    report.deterministic = torch.equal(first, model(ids))
    if not report.deterministic:
        report.failures.append("two evaluation passes give different logits")


def check_call_costs(model, recorded: list[dict], vocab_size: int, device, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """Each recorded kind of training call costs the same per token at batch 1 and 2 and under two seeds."""

    for call in recorded:
        gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)
        ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, call["length"]), generator=gen).to(device)
        costs = []
        for rows, seed in ((1, 0), (2, 0), (1, 1)):
            torch.manual_seed(seed)
            costs.append(flops_per_token(model, ids[:rows], call["kwargs"], training=call["training"], grad=True))
        entry = {**call, "measured": costs}
        report.call_costs.append(entry)
        if max(costs) > 1.01 * min(costs) + 1:
            report.failures.append(f"the cost of model(ids, **{call['kwargs']}) (training={call['training']}) "
                                   f"depends on the batch size or on random draws: {[round(c) for c in costs]} "
                                   "FLOPs per token")


def check_state(model, report: CheckReport) -> None:
    """List state that is not trained by gradient descent; a reviewer decides if it is legitimate."""

    parameter_names = {name for name, _ in model.named_parameters()}
    for name, value in model.state_dict().items():
        if name not in parameter_names:
            report.state_notes.append(f"persistent buffer {name} {tuple(value.shape)} {value.dtype}")
    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
        if not parameter.requires_grad:
            report.state_notes.append(f"frozen parameter {name} {tuple(parameter.shape)}")

python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('experiments/REPORT.md');s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('| Larger FF1240 | 1 | 3.454878 |','| Larger FF1240 | 2 | 3.456208 |')
s=s.replace('| Per-step gains and channel residual/input scales | 1 | 3.442355 |','| Per-step gains and channel residual/input scales | 3 | 3.443176 |')
s += '''
## Validated model at snapshot 011

The selected model at this stage uses a 512-wide tied embedding, one shared 512-wide attention module (8 heads), and one shared SwiGLU module with intermediate width1240. It applies16 recurrent passes. Each pass has its own RMSNorm parameters, per-head Q/K and initial-value gains, per-channel residual scales, and direct initial-input coefficients. The normalized original token embedding is available throughout the recurrence. All attention is causal and uses RoPE. There is no evaluation-time randomness or adaptive computation.

The count is2,994,976 floating-point weights/buffers plus the exempt8192×512 embedding. Inference and training both measure119,668,736 FLOPs/token at512-token windows. Three full-budget seeds have dev losses3.442354540,3.443090036,3.444083474 (mean3.443176017; sample standard deviation0.000867668). The reference mean is3.643780377. Fresh and trained checks pass.

Matrix parameters use Adam moments in periodically refreshed gradient-covariance eigenbases. The optimizer maintains left/right gradient covariances on axes of size at most768, refreshes their eigenvectors every32 updates, and transports the diagonal second moment when the basis changes. The first moment remains in the original coordinates. Embeddings and one-dimensional gain/normalization parameters use AdamW. Both peak learning rates are0.002, with5% warmup and cosine decay to zero. Weight decay is0.1 for matrices/embeddings and zero for gains/normalizations. Batch size is64 windows, each up to512 tokens; global gradient clipping is1. Training uses bfloat16 and compiles the transformer stack and cross-entropy.

These are dev-text results, not a measurement on the two hidden texts. Additional training-recipe and architecture checks are still running. Concurrent wall-clock timings in the CSV are not directly comparable as standalone training-speed measurements.
'''
p.write_text(s)
PY

#326 execpython -u - <<'PY' > runs/finalize.out 2>&1
python -u - <<'PY' > runs/finalize.out 2>&1
import json,math,os,statistics,subprocess,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
Path('runs/finalize.pid').write_text(str(os.getpid()))
candidates={
'gated_b32':['gated_b32_s0','gated_b32_s1'],
'gated_wsd':['gated_wsd_s0','gated_wsd_s1'],
 'two32_raw_b32':['two32_raw_b32_s0','two32_raw_b32_s1']}
deadline=time.monotonic()+540
while not all((Path('runs')/n/'eval.json').exists() for ns in candidates.values() for n in ns):
 if time.monotonic()>deadline:raise TimeoutError('Awaiting final seed results; use completed replicated candidates.')
 time.sleep(2)
means={k:statistics.mean(json.loads((Path('runs')/n/'eval.json').read_text())['loss'] for n in ns) for k,ns in candidates.items()}
assert all(math.isfinite(x) for x in means.values())
best=min(means,key=means.get)
print('Final candidate means:',json.dumps(means),flush=True)
print('Selected',best,flush=True)
subprocess.run([sys.executable,'experiments/finalize.py','--source','experiments/'+best,'--runs',*['runs/'+n for n in candidates[best]]],check=True)
Path('runs/finalization_complete.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(selected=best,means=means),indent=2))
